    <meta name="msapplication-tap-highlight" content="no">
    <style nonce="">
        #community {
            display: none;
        }
        #panel {
            display: none;
        }
        .jbf-init-message {
            display: block;
            position: absolute;
            top: 50%;
            margin-top: -54px;
            left: 0;
            right: 0;
            text-align: center;
            z-index: 5;
        }
        .jbf-init-message .text-message {
            font-size: 1.375rem;
            
            font-weight: 300;
            text-align: center;
            line-height: 1.5rem;
            margin-top: 18px;
            color: #777;
        }
        .jbf-init-message .loading-animated-icon {
            display: inline-block;
        }
        #jbf-fallback-container {
            display: none;
        }
        #jbf-fallback-container .fallback-block-wrapper {
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            bottom: 0;
            left: 0;
            right: 0;
            overflow-x: hidden;
            overflow-y: auto;
            cursor: default;
        }
        #jbf-fallback-container .fallback-block {
            text-align: center;
        }
        #jbf-fallback-container .message {
            font-size: 18px;
        }
        #jbf-fallback-container .actions-list {
            display: block;
            margin-top: 8px;
            margin-bottom: 8px;
        }
        #jbf-fallback-container .actions-list a {
            line-height: 26px;
            display: block;
            cursor: pointer;
        }
        #jbf-fallback-container .actions-list a:active {
            background: #0572ce;
            color: #FFF;
        }
        #jbf-fallback-container .suggestion {
            margin-top: 8px;
            font-size: 14px;
        }
        .hidden {
            display: none;
        }
        .noscript {
            text-align: center;
            display: block;
        }
    </style>

        var listErrorsLoadConfiguration = [];

            return listErrorsLoadConfiguration;

        function defineTextSize() {
            document.body.classList.forEach(function(className){
                if (className.indexOf('a11y-fsz-') !== -1) {
                    document.body.classList.remove(className);
                }
            });

            var bodyFontSize = parseInt(getComputedStyle(document.body).fontSize, 10);

            document.body.classList.toggle('a11y-fsz-' + bodyFontSize, window.isMaybeIos || window.isAndroidApp);
            document.body.classList.toggle('a11y-large-text', bodyFontSize > 17 && window.isAndroidApp || bodyFontSize > 27 && window.isMaybeIos);
            document.body.classList.toggle('a11y-more-than-24', bodyFontSize > 24 && window.isAndroidApp);
            document.body.classList.toggle('a11y-more-than-27', bodyFontSize > 27 && window.isMaybeIos);
            document.body.classList.toggle('a11y-more-than-33', bodyFontSize > 33 && (window.isMaybeIos || window.isAndroidApp));
        }

        // required to know whether maf is already initialized before application starts to create services that rely on MAF API.
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            document.addEventListener("deviceready", function () {
                var nativeOpen = window.open;
                window.open = function (url, target, options) {
                        if (url && url.charAt(0) === '#') {
                            nativeOpen(url, "_system", options); // fix for cordova.InAppBrowser.open("#");
                        } else {
                            nativeOpen(url, target, options);
                        }
                }
                window.isMafReady = true;
                window.isMaf = function () {
                    return true;
                };

                window.isAndroidApp = cordova && cordova.platformId === "android" ? 1 : 0;
                document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', defineTextSize);
                defineTextSize();

                document.dispatchEvent(new Event("mafreadyset"));
            });
        });


        


    </script>

<body class="jet-fix jbf-appheader-offcanvas-pushwrapper webkit webkit53 lt2048 lt2560 hover-enabled themeclaro themeredwood custom-theme dark-header dark-submit light-page bg-color-set mq768 lt1024 lt1280 lt1440 lt1680 lt1920" ofs-version="2504 2507 2510 2601 2604 2607" touch-device="not" style="--true100vh: 1341px;">
    <div id="root" aria-hidden="true"><div></div></div>
    <div id="accessible-title" aria-live="polite" aria-relevant="additions text" aria-hidden="true"><div id="fake-focus-reset" tabindex="0" style="position: absolute; top: 0px; left: -1e+06px;">Oracle Field Service</div></div>
<script nonce="">
    function setThemeCss(themeData) {
        var DEFAULT_BG_VALUE = 'texture';

        themeData = themeData || {};
        var colors = themeData.colors && Array.isArray(themeData.colors) ? themeData.colors : ["#34302E", "#312D2A", DEFAULT_BG_VALUE];//the redwood colors by default
        var isCustomTheme = themeData.isCustom;
        var themeLabel = themeData.label;
        var darkness = themeData.darkness && Array.isArray(themeData.darkness) ? themeData.darkness : [1, 1, 0];//the redwood colors by default
        var bgColorIsSet = colors && colors[2] && colors[2] !== DEFAULT_BG_VALUE;
        //set CSS variables vith theme colors
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--theme-header-background-color', colors && colors[0] || "#34302E");
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--theme-button-background-color', colors && colors[1] || "#312D2A");
        if (bgColorIsSet) {
            document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--theme-page-background-color', colors[2]);
        } else {
            document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--theme-page-background-color', 'transparent');
        }

        //remove all "theme******" CSS classes
        document.body.classList.forEach(function(className){
            if (className.indexOf('theme') !== -1) {
                document.body.classList.remove(className);
            }
        });

        document.body.classList.add('themeredwood'); //TODO It is a mistake "themeredwood" shouldn't be added with "custom-theme" but it is legacy!!!

        //add custom theme class
        if (isCustomTheme) {
            document.body.classList.add(themeLabel);
            document.body.classList.add('custom-theme');
        }

        //update classes that is responsible for darkness
        [
            'dark-header',
            'dark-page',
            'dark-submit',
            'light-header',
            'light-page',
            'light-submit',
            'bg-color-set'
        ].forEach(function (className) {
            document.body.classList.remove(className);
        });

        if (darkness[0]) {
            document.body.classList.add('dark-header');
        } else {
            document.body.classList.add('light-header');
        }

        if (darkness[1]) {
            document.body.classList.add('dark-submit');
        } else {
            document.body.classList.add('light-submit');
        }

        if (darkness[2]) {
            document.body.classList.add('dark-page');
        } else {
            document.body.classList.add('light-page');
        }
        bgColorIsSet && document.body.classList.add('bg-color-set');
    }

    try {
        setThemeCss({"darkness":[1,1,0],"colors":["#D52B1E","#0572ce","#fafafa"],"isCustom":1,"label":"themeclaro","text":"Claro.ETAdirect"});
    } catch (e){
        console.error(e);
    }

</script>
<noscript class="noscript">Por favor habilite JavaScript en su navegador</noscript>

<style nonce="">
    body.self-heal-checking {
        font-family: 'Oracle Sans', sans-serif;
        font-size: 1rem;
        background-color: rgb(252, 251, 250);
    }
    #wait {
        display: flex;
        align-content: center;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        text-align: center;
        left: 50%;
        top: 50%;
        transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
        z-index: 1;
        position: fixed;
    }
    #wait .text-message {
        font-size   : 1.375rem;
        font-family : Arial, Helvetica, Roboto, sans-serif !important;
        font-stretch: condensed;
        font-weight : 300 !important;
        text-align  : center;
        line-height : 1.5rem;
        margin-top  : 18px;
        color       : rgba(22, 21, 19, 0.65);
    }
    #wait_text {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
    }
    #wait .loading-animated-icon {
        display: inline-block;
    }

    .user-confirm {
        position: absolute;
        z-index: 2;
        left: 0;
        right: 0;
        top: 0;
        bottom: 0;
        overflow: auto;
        min-width: 320px;
        background: #306170 url('../r2607.971.26.20.40513/mobile/css/../images/themeredwood/jpeg/Abstracts_Blue_2.jpg') center center no-repeat;
        background-size: cover;
        font-family: 'Oracle Sans';
        color: #fcfbfa;
        display: -webkit-box;
        display: -ms-flexbox;
        display: flex;
        -webkit-box-align: start;
        -ms-flex-align: start;
        align-items: start;
        -webkit-box-pack: center;
        -ms-flex-pack: center;
        justify-content: center;
    }
    .user-confirm-content {
        -webkit-box-orient: vertical;
        -webkit-box-direction: normal;
        -ms-flex-direction: column;
        flex-direction: column;
        width: 300px;
        margin: 215px 0 40px 0;
    }
    .user-confirm-header {
        font-size: 20px;
        font-weight: bold;
    }
    .user-confirm-text {
        margin: 32px 0px 8px;
        font-size: 14px;
        font-weight: normal;
    }
    .user-confirm-button {
        font-family: 'Oracle Sans';
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: 14px;
        border: 1px solid #A1a09F;
        border-radius: 4px;
        color: #1A1816;
        background: #FFF;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        padding: 8px 16px;
        height: 36px;
        min-width: 88px;
    }
    .user-confirm-button:hover, .user-confirm-button:focus {
        background-color: rgba(22, 21, 19, 0.04);
        border-color: rgba(22, 21, 19, 0.6);
    }
    .user-confirm-button:focus {
        border-color: rgba(22, 21, 19, 0.6);
        outline: 1px dotted #161513;
        outline-offset: 1px;
    }
    .user-confirm-button:disabled {
        border-color: transparent;
        color: rgba(22, 21, 19, 0.3);
        background: rgba(123, 117, 112, 0.1);
        box-shadow: none;
    }
    .user-confirm-button.submit {
        color: #FFFFFF;
        background: rgb(67, 107, 29);
        border: none;
    }
    .user-confirm-button.submit:hover {
        background-color: #577346;
        background-image: linear-gradient(rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15), rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15));
        border-color: transparent;
    }
    .user-confirm-button.submit:active {
        background-color: #4F693F;
    }
    .user-confirm-button.submit:disabled {
        background-color: #FCFBFA;
        color: #D2D0CF;
    }
    .user-confirm-button.submit:focus {
        background-color: #4F693F;
        outline: 1px dotted #161513;
        outline-offset: 1px;
    }
    .user-confirm-button:focus:not(:disabled) {
        box-shadow: none;
    }

    .loading-animated-icon {
        width: 32px;
        height: 32px;
        overflow: hidden;
        position: relative;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon .part1 {
        display: block;
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        width: 15px;
        height: 15px;
        border-top-left-radius: 32px;
        border: 1px solid #777;
        border-bottom: 0 none;
        border-right: 0 none;
        -webkit-transform-origin: 16px 16px;
        transform-origin: 16px 16px;
        -webkit-animation: loading-animated-icon-rotating 1.2s linear infinite;
        animation: loading-animated-icon-rotating 1.2s linear infinite;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon .part2 {
        display: block;
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 4px;
        right: 4px;
        width: 11px;
        height: 11px;
        border-bottom-right-radius: 32px;
        border: 1px solid #777;
        border-top: 0 none;
        border-left: 0 none;
        -webkit-transform-origin: left top;
        transform-origin: left top;
        -webkit-animation: loading-animated-icon-rotating-backwards 1s linear infinite;
        animation: loading-animated-icon-rotating-backwards 1s linear infinite;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon.none-animation-default .part1 {
        -webkit-animation: none;
        animation: none;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon.none-animation-default .part2 {
        -webkit-animation: none;
        animation: none;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon.none-animation-default.start-animation .part1 {
        -webkit-animation: loading-animated-icon-rotating 0.7s linear infinite;
        animation: loading-animated-icon-rotating 0.7s linear infinite;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon.none-animation-default.start-animation .part2 {
        -webkit-animation: loading-animated-icon-rotating-backwards 0.5s linear infinite;
        animation: loading-animated-icon-rotating-backwards 0.5s linear infinite;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon.loading-start .part1 {
        border-color: #777;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon.loading-start .part2 {
        border-color: #777;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon.loading-wait .part1 {
        border-color: #777;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon.loading-wait .part2 {
        border-color: #777;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon.big {
        width: 64px;
        height: 64px;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon.big .part1 {
        width: 31px;
        height: 31px;
        border-top-left-radius: 64px;
        border-width: 1px;
        -webkit-transform-origin: 32px 32px;
        transform-origin: 32px 32px;
    }
    .loading-animated-icon.big .part2 {
        bottom: 4px;
        right: 4px;
        width: 27px;
        height: 27px;
        border-bottom-right-radius: 64px;
        border-width: 1px;
    }

    @-webkit-keyframes loading-animated-icon-rotating {
        100% {
            -webkit-transform: rotate(360deg);
            transform: rotate(360deg);
        }
    }
    @-webkit-keyframes loading-animated-icon-rotating-backwards {
        100% {
            -webkit-transform: rotate(-360deg);
            transform: rotate(-360deg);
        }
    }
    @keyframes loading-animated-icon-rotating {
        100% {
            -webkit-transform: rotate(360deg);
            transform: rotate(360deg);
        }
    }
    @keyframes loading-animated-icon-rotating-backwards {
        100% {
            -webkit-transform: rotate(-360deg);
            transform: rotate(-360deg);
        }
    }

    .hidden {
        display: none;
    }
</style>

<div id="wait" style="display: none;" aria-hidden="true">

    <oj-messages id="appMessages" messages="[[toastMessagesDataProvider]]" display="notification" display-options.category="none" role="complementary" position="[[toastMessagesPosition]]" class="oj-messages-notification oj-complete" aria-label="Mensajes"><div role="presentation" :id="[[containerId]]" :class="[[containerSelectors]]" on-oj-open="[[handleOpen]]" on-oj-close="[[handleClose]]" on-oj-animate-start="[[handleAnimateStart]]" class="oj-messages-container" id="_oj0_mc">  <!--oj-bind-if test='[[!$properties.messages]]'--><!--ko _ojBindIf_V2_:!$properties.messages--><!--/ko--><!--/oj-bind-if-->  <!--oj-bind-if test='[[$properties.messages]]'--><!--ko _ojBindIf_V2_:$properties.messages-->    <!--oj-bind-for-each data='[[$properties.messages]]'--><!--ko _ojBindForEach_:{data:$properties.messages}--><!--/ko--><!--/oj-bind-for-each-->  <!--/ko--><!--/oj-bind-if--></div><div data-bind="_ojNodeStorage_" class="oj-subtree-hidden" style="display: none;"><template slot="messageTemplate" data-oj-as="message">
            <oj-message message="[[message.data]]" display-options.category="none">
                <div slot="detail" class="oj-flex oj-sm-flex-direction-column">
                    <!-- The detail text area -->
                    <div class="oj-flex-item">
                      <span>
                        <oj-bind-text value="[[message.data.detail]]"></oj-bind-text>
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <!-- Actions -->
                    <oj-bind-if test="[[message.data.actions]]">
                        <div class="oj-flex oj-flex-item oj-sm-flex-items-initial oj-sm-padding-2x-top">
                            <oj-bind-for-each data="[[message.data.actions]]">
                                <template>
                                    <div class="oj-flex-item oj-sm-margin-5x-end">
                                        <a data-bind="fastClick: $current.data.action" class="oj-link-standalone oj-typography-body-sm oj-typography-semi-bold">
                                            <oj-bind-text value="[[$current.data.title]]"></oj-bind-text>
                                        </a>
                                    </div>
                                </template>
                            </oj-bind-for-each>
                        </div>
                    </oj-bind-if>
                </div>
            </oj-message>
        </template></div></oj-messages>

                        },

                                    'oj-ux-ico-caret-up': (isTitleActionIndicatorActive() &amp;&amp; !isNewHeaderFeatureEnabled()),
                                    'oj-ux-ico-nav-down': (!isTitleActionIndicatorActive() &amp;&amp; isNewHeaderFeatureEnabled()),

            <div class="page-header-actions-region" data-bind="visible: isVisibleToolbarActions">
                <controls:toolbar class="page-header-actions ko:controls:toolbar" data-ofsc-role="page-header-actions" params="
                        models: toolbarActionModels,
                        modelsAutoDispose: toolbarActionModelsAutoDispose,
                        instanceHolder: toolbarInstanceHolder,
                        menuButtonTitle: menuButtonTitle,
                        isMenuIndicatorHidden: true
                    "><div class="toolbar-main-row" data-ofsc-role="toolbar">
    <div class="toolbar-items-list" data-bind="
            foreach: {data: items, as: 'item', noChildContext: true},
            childrenComplete: associateComponentsAndElements.bind($component),
            css: { 'toolbar-items-list--no-menu-button': isMenuButtonHidden }
        ">
        <div class="toolbar-item" data-ofsc-role="toolbar-item" data-bind="
                visible: item.model.visibleObservable,
                insertComponent: { name: item.componentName, params: '{model: item.model}'},
                css: { 'toolbar-item--does-not-fit': item.doesNotFit },

    <div class="oj-flex-item oj-sm-12 search-bar-input-wrap-container" role="search">
        <div class="search-bar-input-wrap">

            <div class="search-bar-input-element-wrap" role="textbox" aria-label="Search input">
                <div class="search-bar-input-hint" data-bind="fastClick: {method: onClick, blockOtherGestures: true}, visible:!searchInputToggler()">
                    <div class="search-bar-input-icon"></div>
                    <div class="search-bar-input-hint-text" data-bind="text:hintText">Buscar en actividades</div>
                </div>

                <input placeholder="" type="search" autocorrect="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false" class="search-bar-input" tabindex="0" data-bind="textInput:searchValue,  event: { keyup: onKeyPressSearch , paste: performImmediateSearch }, hasFocus:isSearchInFocus, attr:{'aria-label': hintText}" aria-label="Buscar en actividades">
            </div>

            <div role="button" class="search-bar-input-barcode-scanner ofs_ico_scan_barcode" aria-label="Barcode Scanner" tabindex="0" data-bind="visible: barcodeScannerService.isServiceAvailable() &amp;&amp; !searchInputToggler(), fastClick: {method: openBarCodeScanner.bind($data), blockOtherGestures: true}" style="display: none;"></div>

            <div aria-busy="true" class="search-bar-input-loading" data-bind="visible: searchInputToggler() &amp;&amp; currentSearchState() == SEARCH_STATE.IN_PROGRESS" style="display: none;"></div>

            <div role="button" class="search-bar-input-clear" aria-label="Clear Search" tabindex="0" data-bind="visible: searchInputToggler() &amp;&amp; currentSearchState() != SEARCH_STATE.IN_PROGRESS, fastClick: {method: searchInputClear.bind($data), blockOtherGestures: true}, event: {keypress: resetSearch.bind($data)}" style="display: none;"></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ko if: [SEARCH_STATE.INITIAL, SEARCH_STATE.ACTIVATED, SEARCH_STATE.IN_PROGRESS, SEARCH_STATE.COMPLETED].indexOf(currentSearchState()) >= 0 -->

    <div class="oj-flex-item">
    <div class="search-bar-navigate">
        <a data-bind="fastClick: goToPreference, event:{keypress:goToPreference}, attr:{'title': i18n(11858 ) }" tabindex="0" title="Buscar preferencias">
            <span aria-hidden="true"></span>
        </a>
    </div>
    </div>
    <!-- /ko -->

    <div class="oj-flex-item global-search-results">
        <div class="oj-flex-item global-search-info-displays">
            <div data-bind="renderer: renderInfo.bind($data)"><div class="info-display-wrapper parts-catalog is-empty"><div class="info-display-header"><div class="info-display-header-wrapper" tabindex="0"><div class="info-display-header-title">Catálogo de piezas fuera de línea</div><div class="info-display-header-status"> - Inicialización</div></div></div><div class="info-display-details-body"></div></div></div>
        </div>
        <!-- ko if: [SEARCH_STATE.ACTIVATED, SEARCH_STATE.INITIAL].indexOf(currentSearchState()) >= 0 && recentSearchResults().length != 0 --><!-- /ko -->

        <div class="oj-flex-item oj-sm-12 global-search-results-message-container">
            <!--oj-bind-if test='[[message]]'--><!--ko _ojBindIf_V2_:message--><!--/ko--><!--/oj-bind-if-->
        </div>

        <!-- ko if: [SEARCH_STATE.ACTIVATED, SEARCH_STATE.IN_PROGRESS, SEARCH_STATE.COMPLETED].indexOf(currentSearchState()) >= 0 --><!-- /ko -->

    </div>
</div></global-services:global-search:global-search-bar>
